snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/tests/lowering/r1p-sources.jsonl:567:["c15abe3c30e40b2cd9e73207cf53bdc3db465b619bd5eb50d58e01c0ca1e4187",[["schema.q","\ntable records:\n  key: text <=80\n  owner: principal\n  secret: text <=4000\n  frozen: text <=80\n  amount: int\n  photo: image <=1000 ?\n  unique byKey: key\n  by byOwner: owner, id\n  immutable frozen\n  read <- exists grants[.id, viewer]\n  insert <- .owner = viewer\n  update <- exists grants[.id, viewer]\nmutation edit(key: text <=80):\n  return update records[key = key] {amount: 0}\nmutation editId(target: records):\n  return update records[id = target] {amount: 0}\nmutation conditional(key: text <=80, expected: int):\n  return update records[key = key] {amount: 0} if .amount = expected\nmutation immutable(key: text <=80, guess: text <=80):\n  return update records[key = key] {frozen: guess}\nmutation put(key: text <=80):\n  return upsert records[key = key] {amount: 0}\nmutation full(key: text <=80):\n  return upsert records[key = key] {owner: 'alice', secret: 'supplied', frozen: 'fixed', amount: 0}\nmutation add(key: text <=80):\n  return insert records {key, owner: viewer, secret: 'supplied', frozen: 'fixed', amount: 0}\nmutation quiet(key: text <=80):\n  return insert records {key, owner: viewer, secret: 'supplied', frozen: 'fixed', amount: 0} if absent\nquery one(key: text <=80):\n  return records[key = key]\n\ntable grants:\n  record: records\n  principal: principal\n  unique byPair: record, principal\n  read <- allow\ntable work:\n  record: records\n  read <- exists grants[.record, viewer]\nmutation warm(pad: text <=4000, key: text <=80, target: records): server\n  for i in range(0, 8192):\n    w = work[id = 'work']\n  return null\nmutation pressure(pad: text <=4000, key: text <=80, target: records): server\n  for i in range(0, 8192):\n    w = work[id = 'work']\n  upsert records[key = key] {amount: 0}\n"]]]
snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/tests/lowering/r1p-sources.jsonl:572:["c2db1a98f8c4aee2bfa2bd5b7aa80773921f7e9a9258320dac072ad354d915eb",[["schema.q","table memberships:\n  chatKey: text 1..40\n  principal: principal\n  role: enum('admin', 'member')\n  unique byChatPrincipal: chatKey, principal\n  by byPrincipalChat: principal, chatKey, id\n  cap 16 by chatKey\n  cap 16 by principal\n  read <- exists memberships[chatKey = .chatKey, principal = viewer]\n  sync\n\ntable messages:\n  chatKey: text 1..40\n  sender: principal\n  read <- exists memberships[.chatKey, viewer]\n\ntable acknowledgments:\n  messageId: messages\n  chatKey: text 1..40\n  principal: principal\n  state: enum('delivered', 'read')\n  unique byMessagePrincipal: messageId, principal\n  by byMessagePrincipalState: messageId, principal, state\n  read <- exists memberships[.chatKey, viewer]\n\nquery roster(chatKey: text 1..40):\n  return memberships[chatKey] first 16 by byChatPrincipal\n\nquery directory():\n  return memberships[viewer] first 16 by byPrincipalChat\n\nquery receipt(messageId: messages):\n  msg = messages[messageId]\n  if msg = null:\n    return null\n  recipients = memberships[msg.chatKey] first 16 by byChatPrincipal where .principal != msg.sender {\n    read: 1 if exists acknowledgments[messageId = msg.id, principal = .principal, state = 'read'] by byMessagePrincipalState else 0,\n    delivered: 1 if exists acknowledgments[messageId = msg.id, principal = .principal, state = 'delivered'] by byMessagePrincipalState or exists acknowledgments[messageId = msg.id, principal = .principal, state = 'read'] by byMessagePrincipalState else 0\n  }\n  if sum(recipients, .read) = count(recipients):\n    return { state: 'read' }\n  if sum(recipients, .delivered) = count(recipients):\n    return { state: 'delivered' }\n  return { state: 'sent' }\n"]]]
snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/tests/lowering/r1p-sources.jsonl:582:["c624af0adfc8f11765bed02b4953208e79289703b7851446e50aa80388aaadfa",[["schema.q","effect notify\ntable notes:\n  n: int\n  unique byN: n\n  public 'notes'\n  sync public\n  insert <- allow\n  update <- allow\n  delete <- allow\n\nmutation probe(p: principal):\n  delete notes[n = connected[p]]\n"]]]

snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/tests/lowering/r1p-sources.jsonl:689:["eca496235cee2755cc680d33f0dd479b9c659cdcd3320deef598ffbc0bcbe1b1",[["schema.q","\ntable records:\n  key: text <=80\n  owner: principal\n  secret: text <=80\n  frozen: text <=80\n  amount: int\n  photo: image <=1000 ?\n  unique byKey: key\n  by byOwner: owner, id\n  immutable frozen\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  insert <- .owner = viewer\n  update <- allow\n  sync to .owner last 100 by byOwner\nmutation edit(key: text <=80):\n  return update records[key = key] {amount: 0}\nmutation editId(target: records):\n  return update records[id = target] {amount: 0}\nmutation conditional(key: text <=80, expected: int):\n  return update records[key = key] {amount: 0} if .amount = expected\nmutation immutable(key: text <=80, guess: text <=80):\n  return update records[key = key] {frozen: guess}\nmutation put(key: text <=80):\n  return upsert records[key = key] {amount: 0}\nmutation full(key: text <=80):\n  return upsert records[key = key] {owner: 'alice', secret: 'supplied', frozen: 'fixed', amount: 0}\nmutation add(key: text <=80):\n  return insert records {key, owner: viewer, secret: 'supplied', frozen: 'fixed', amount: 0}\nmutation quiet(key: text <=80):\n  return insert records {key, owner: viewer, secret: 'supplied', frozen: 'fixed', amount: 0} if absent\nquery one(key: text <=80):\n  return records[key = key]\n\nmutation profile(key: text <=80):\n  return upsert records[key = key] {owner: viewer, secret: 'supplied', frozen: 'wrong', amount: 0}\n"]]]

   213	The binder also marks an ephemeral mutation **`renews`** only when it can
   214	prove that running it twice in a row has the same effect and result as
   215	running it once. The proof is conservative:
   216	
   217	- the program makes exactly one write, an `upsert` or `update` of an
   218	  ephemeral table;
   219	- that write's key and every patch value are built only from arguments,
   220	  `viewer`, literals and `now`, never from a read (for example, no
   221	  `n: old.n + 1`);
   222	- anything before the write is a `require` guard, or a `let` used only by
   223	  guards; those guards may read durable tables only, since an ephemeral
   224	  write may invalidate an ephemeral guard;
   225	- the program returns nothing.
   226	
   227	`setTyping` renews. None of these does: `stopTyping`, an insert of a
   228	reaction burst, a counter or toggle, or any operation that returns a value.
   229	(Revision 3: Astra's delta probe compiled an upsert keyed by `viewer` that
   230	patched `n: old.n + 1`. It met revision 2's key-only criterion but was not a
   231	renewal.) Only `renews` operations are coalesced or suppressed by the
   232	client (§2.4). All others are sent once per call, in order.
   233	
   234	The classification appears in three places:
   235	
   236	- `check`'s program listing, as `ephemeral` or `ephemeral, renews`;
   237	- `check --residency`, where each ephemeral table is listed as
   238	  `ephemeral <life>: server memory, never on a device`;
   239	- the generated operation (§2.4).
   240	
   241	An ephemeral mutation's semantics:
   242	
   243	- **Life.** Each insert or upsert-insert of an ephemeral row stamps the
   244	  deadline `now + life`. Each update, and each upsert that patches an
   245	  existing row, stamps it again, even with an empty patch. That renewal is
   246	  what a heartbeat means. `$deadline` is stamped as its own assignment:
   247	  `recompute_deadline` returns early without `through`, and `merge_patch`
   248	  copies the old deadline. A delete removes the row.
   249	- **The renewal law.** Any write that stamps `$deadline` must pass the
   250	  table's **insert** rule on the new image, in addition to whatever rule
   251	  the write already needs (update, or insert on the insert arm). Extending a
   252	  row's life re-admits it. So a principal who loses the right to insert,
   253	  for example a removed member, cannot keep a row alive past one life,
   254	  whatever the update rule says. Refusal is the update's ordinary refusal
   255	  (`E_RULE` / `NOT_FOUND` per the writes card's table).
   256	- **Rules.** Read, insert, update and delete rules are checked exactly as
   501	### 3.2 Authorization and revocation
   502	
   503	- Ephemeral rows are read under their table's read rule, like any row.
   504	- Rules are evaluated at read time, and ephemeral rows are never cached on a
   505	  device. So a principal who loses membership sees nothing on their next
   506	  read of that room.
   507	- A durable commit to a table a watched query probes invalidates that
   508	  query, as today.
   509	- By the renewal law (§2.2), a departed member's rows outlive the departure
   510	  by at most one life, whatever the update rule says.
   511	- **Disclosure.** The `/changes` answer names ephemeral tables that were
   512	  written. That is the same class as today's table-granular durable
   513	  invalidation, which names every table a commit touched (`serve_changes.rs:80-94`).
   514	  It adds no new leak class. Capacity refusals depend only on the writer's
   515	  own rows (§3.5), except for the global backstop, which reveals only a
   516	  server-wide aggregate, like HTTP admission.

   147	pub fn sections(card: &str) -> Vec<(usize, String, usize, usize)> {
   148	    let lines: Vec<&str> = card.lines().collect();
   149	    let mut fenced = false;
   150	    let mut found: Vec<(usize, String, usize, usize)> = Vec::new();
   151	    for (index, line) in lines.iter().enumerate() {
   152	        if line.trim_start().starts_with("```") { fenced = !fenced; continue; }
   153	        if fenced { continue; }
   154	        let level = line.chars().take_while(|c| *c == '#').count();
   155	        if !(2..=3).contains(&level) || !line[level..].starts_with(' ') { continue; }
   156	        found.push((level, line[level..].trim().to_owned(), index, lines.len()));
   157	    }
   158	    // A section runs to the next heading at its level or above.
   159	    for i in 0..found.len() {
   160	        let level = found[i].0;
   161	        if let Some(next) = found[i + 1..].iter().find(|next| next.0 <= level) { found[i].3 = next.2; }
   162	    }
   163	    found
   164	}

   176	fn words_of(text: &str) -> String {
   177	    text.chars().map(|c| if c.is_alphanumeric() { c.to_ascii_lowercase() } else { ' ' }).collect::<String>().split_whitespace().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" ")

   333	pub fn section(name: &str, card: &str, words: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
   334	    let found = sections(card);
   335	    let lines: Vec<&str> = card.lines().collect();
   336	    let title = lines.first().filter(|line| line.starts_with("# ")).map(|line| format!("{line}\n\n")).unwrap_or_default();
   337	    let wanted = words_of(words);
   338	    let print = |start: usize, end: usize| format!("{title}{}", lines[start..end].join("\n").trim_end());
   339	    let opening = opening_end(card, &found);
   340	    if matches!(wanted.as_str(), "0" | "opening" | "intro" | "introduction") {
   341	        return Ok(lines[..opening].join("\n").trim_end().to_owned());
   342	    }

   344	        return match found.get(n.wrapping_sub(1)) {

   346	            // `1000` is an alias of the bounds card, not its thousandth section.
   347	            None => alias_inside(name, card, words).ok_or_else(|| format!("guide {name} has no section {n}\n{}", section_index(name, card))),
   348	        };
   349	    }

   368	        }
   369	    }

// W14b revision 2 §6: count physical lines and require the in-file first-screen path.
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

const root = new URL("../", import.meta.url);
const budget = JSON.parse(readFileSync(new URL("metrics/docs-budget.json", root), "utf8"));
const readme = readFileSync(new URL("packages/snapback4/README.md", root), "utf8");
const actual = (readme.match(/\n/g)?.length ?? 0) + Number(readme.length > 0 && !readme.endsWith("\n"));
const valid = Number.isSafeInteger(budget.readmeLines) && budget.readmeLines > 0 &&
  typeof budget.set === "string" && budget.set.length > 0 && typeof budget.why === "string" && budget.why.length > 0;
console.log(`docs budget: README ${actual}/${budget.readmeLines} physical lines`);
if (!valid) console.error("docs budget: expected a positive readmeLines budget, set date and reason");
if (actual > budget.readmeLines) console.error("docs budget: README exceeds its budget; preserve the first-screen path when editing");
// Run even after a size failure: the suite reports both failures, and size alone cannot qualify the orientation.
const sufficient = spawnSync("cargo", ["test", "-p", "snapback4-lang", "--test", "guide", "w14b_first_account_screen_is_self_contained", "--", "--exact"],
  { cwd: fileURLToPath(root), encoding: "utf8" });
process.stdout.write(sufficient.stdout ?? "");
process.stderr.write(sufficient.stderr ?? "");
const ran = /test result: ok\. 1 passed; 0 failed;/.test(sufficient.stdout ?? "");
if (sufficient.status === 0 && !ran) console.error("docs budget: the named self-containment test must run exactly once");
if (sufficient.error) console.error(`docs budget: self-containment test could not run: ${sufficient.error.message}`);
